Projection onto simplex sum=1, nonnegative:
def project_simplex(v, target=1):
    # v array
    # solve max(0, v - τ) sum=target. Find τ by sorting.
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * (np.arange(1, len(u)+1)) > (cssv - target))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.zeros_like(v)
    tau = (cssv[rho[-1]] - target)/(rho[-1]+1)
    return np.maximum(v - tau, 0)

Let's detail optimize_sequence(a_init, time_budget, best_score=None):
```python
def optimize_sequence(a_init, budget_s, target_stop=0.0, seed=None):
    a = np.asarray(a_init,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return...
    a = a/s
    n = len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_from_b(conv_a(a))? compute b.

Function gradient_weights_fft(A, weights, L, n):
```
P = rfft(weights, L)
c2 = ifft(A*P).real[:3*n-2]
ph = 2*np.pi*np.arange(L//2+1)*(2*n-2)/L  # P_len-1 = 2n-2? P length = 2n-1, P-1=2n-2.
Pr = P * (cos(ph) - 1j*sin(ph))
c1 = ifft(Pr? Wait need A*Pr)
c1 = ifft(A*Pr).real[:3*n-2]
grad = c2[:n] + c1[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
return grad
```
Check phase formula: r = 2n-2. For k=0..L//2, Pr[k] = P[k] * exp(-2πi k r/L). But for rfft only positive freqs; ifft expects full symmetric? For real sequence reversed, we can multiply rfft result by phase and ifft with hermitian? Does multiplying rfft positive freqs by phase produce correct inverse for real? The rfft output excludes negative frequencies, but ifft assumes conjugate symmetry for input. If we modify positive freqs but not negative, the result may be complex not corresponding to real reversed. However for convolution with real p_rev, we need full FFT of p_rev. The formula for full DFT holds for all frequencies. If we only update positive and leave negative implied by conjugate, is that correct if p_rev real? The phase factor for negative frequencies would be conjugate, and rfft ifft reconstructs negative as conjugate of positive input. If we set positive = P_pos * phase_pos, then negative implied = conj(P_pos * phase_pos) = P_neg * conj(phase_pos). But desired P_neg = P_neg_input? For original p, P_neg=conj(P_pos). For reversed, desired full = P_orig_full * phase_full. At negative freq -k: P_orig_neg * phase_neg = conj(P_pos) * conj(phase_pos) = conj(P_pos*phase_pos). So implied negative matches. Good. So multiplying rfft by phase works.
Need phase = np.exp(-1j*2π*k*r/L). Use cos/sin.

Let's design family scan function:
```
def generalized_p(n, p):
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(i-1+p)/i
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
    return a
```
For p large (e.g., 2), coefficients ~i^{p-1}/Γ(p), increasing; sum ~n^p/p. Recurrence stable for n up to 8192? p=2, a_i ~i, value 8191, sum ~n^2/2, normalization small but fine. Recurrence no overflow for p up to maybe 5: a last ~n^{p-1}/Γ(p) ~8191^4/24~2e12, fine. Double okay. Could include p up to 2.0. But p too high maybe sequence increasing (mass at right), objective maybe? Reversal symmetric, same as decreasing? Actually p>1 coefficients increasing, which is reverse of decreasing? Reversal of coefficients of (1-z^{-1})? Score invariant under reversal, so p>1 equivalent to something? If a_i increasing, reverse is decreasing with different p? Not same family. But includes asymm. def modulate(base, freq, amp, phase, n):
    t=np.arange(n)/n
    w = 1 + amp*np.sin(2π*freq*t + phase)
    w=np.clip(w, 1e-3, None)
    a=base*w; normalize
```

Function `score_fft(a)`:
```
def score_fft(a):
    n = a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.inf
    L = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:2*n-1]
    # clean
    b = np.where(b < 0, 0.0, b)
    max_b = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(max_b): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*max_b/(s*s))
```
But in optimizer we already compute b with L=3n-2 for gradient; for scans use smaller L maybe. Use separate `convolution_fft(a)` with L next_fast_len(2n-1) for score. That's fine.

Use `scipy.fft.next_fast_len` if available. Transforms: use `np.fft.rfft` (maybe okay) or `scipy.fft.rfft` (similar). Use numpy for simplicity.

Weights function:
```
def make_weights_from_b(b, mode, q, n):
    if b.size ==0: return None
    if mode[0]=='p':
       x = b.copy()
    else:
       c = 1.0/(2*n-1)
       x = b - c
       x = np.maximum(x,0.0)
       if x.sum() <= 1e-300:
           x = b*b
    xmax = float(x.max())
    if not np.isfinite(xmax) or xmax <= 0:
       # uniform over all b
       w = np.full_like(x, 1.0/x.size)
       return w
    r = x / xmax
    if q == 1:
       w = r
    else:
       with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
           w = np.power(r, q)
       w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s = w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       w = np.zeros_like(x)
       w[np.argmax(x)] = 1.0
       return w
    return w / s
```
For mode power with q=2, x=b, xmax max; r≤1. q integer. Good.

Could use `np.float64`.

- If budget_s <=0 return.

- For each stage:
   stage_end = time.monotonic()+stage_budget; if <=now skip.
   m=v=zeros; t=0; last_improve=0; iter=0
   lr = 0.05 if q<16 else 0.02
   while now < stage_end:
      iter++
      a = softmax(z)
      A = rfft(a,L)
      b = ifft(A*A).real[:P]
      b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
      max_b = b.max(); sum_a=a.sum(); score=2n max_b/(sum_a^2)
      if score < best_score - 1e-12: best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=iter
      # optional if score finite and best_stop? if best_score < best_target? We still continue maybe.
      if iter % 50 ==0? Maybe no.
      w = make_weights(b, mode,q,n)
      grad = gradient_weights(A,w,n,L,phase)
      gy = a * (grad - float(np.dot(a, grad)))
      # clean gy: finite, clip
      gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)
      z,m,v,t = adam_update(z, gy, m, v, t, lr, 0.9, 0.999, 1e-12)
      z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
      z -= z.mean()
      # noise
      if rng is not None and (iter - last_improve) > 2000 and iter % 1000 == 0:
          z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n)
          z = np.clip(z, -50, 50); z -= z.mean()
      if (iter - last_improve) > 10000: break
   # after stage maybe record
```

Let's outline code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len
except Exception:
    def next_fast_len(n):
       m=1
       while m<n: m*=2
       return m
try:
    import scipy.fft as spfft
    rfft = spfft.rfft
    ifft = spfft.ifft
except:
    rfft = np.fft.rfft
    ifft = np.fft.ifft
```

`make_modulated(base, freq, amp, phase)`:
```
n=base.size; t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
w=1.0+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*t+phase)
w=np.maximum(w,1e-6)
a=base*w; return normalize(a)
```
Could produce slightly better.

`make_exponent_ramp(base, beta)`:
```
t=(np.arange(n)- (n-1)/2)/( (n-1)/2+1)
a=base*np.exp(beta*t); normalize clip
```

`resample_sequence(a,n_new)`:
```
if a.size == n_new: return a.copy()/sum
t_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
t_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
# ensure endpoints
val=np.interp(t_new, t_old, loga)
val -= val.max()
out=np.exp(val)
out /= out.sum()
return out
```

Let's write helpers:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(x):
    a=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       a=np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    else: a=a/s
    return a
```
For external return, maybe we don't want clip 1000? Values <1; okay.

`make_generalized_p(n, p)`:
```
a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*((i-1)+p)/i
return normalize_nonnegative(a)
```
If p large, a values large but normalize; no overflow for n=8192,p=2: a~n/Γ2=8191, fine. p=5: a~n^4/24 ~2e12, fine. p=10: n^9/9! ~1e34/3.6e6=3e27, still below 1e308. Could include p up to 3. Use p_list [0.5,0.55,0.6,0.65,0.7,0.8,0.9,1.0,1.2,1.5].

```
t=base.copy()
t += rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
return normalize_nonnegative(t)
```

Candidate pool function:
```
def build_candidate_pool(n, extra_arrays=None, seed=1234):
    pool=[]
    p_values = [...]
    for p in p_values: pool.append((make_generalized_p(n,p), 'p%.3f'%p)) # no f-string? can use format
    base = make_generalized_p(n,0.5)
    for freq in [1,2,3,5,8]:
       for amp in [0.3,0.6]:
          pool.append((make_modulated(base, float(freq), amp, 0.0), 'mod%d_a%.1f' % (freq, amp)))
    for beta in [-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       pool.append((make_ramp(base,beta),'ramp%.1f'%beta))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + n)
    for sigma in [0.2,0.5]:
       a = base + sigma*... maybe log lognormal: a=base*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)); normalize
       pool.append((a,'rand%.1f'%sigma))
    if extra_arrays:
       idx=0
       for ea in extra_arrays:
          if ea is not None and len(ea)>0:
             pool.append((normalize_nonnegative(ea), 'extra%d'%idx))
             idx+=1
    return pool
```

Adam update function:
```
def adam_update(z, g, m, v, t, lr, b1, b2, eps):
    t += 1
    m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*g
    v = b2*v + (1.0-b2)*(g*g)
    bc1 = 1.0 - b1**t
    bc2 = 1.0 - b2**t
    z = z - lr * (m/bc1) / (np.sqrt(v/bc2) + eps)
    return z,m,v,t
```
`b1**t` each iter slow; can update bias factors incrementally? Not necessary. For 10000 iters, pow in Python maybe okay but can optimize: maintain `bias1=b1`, `bias2=b2`? Simpler use formulas. Could use `np.power`? scalar pow okay. To avoid, we can just not bias-correct after first? Adam without bias: z -= lr * m / (sqrt(v)+eps). Since m,v rapidly converge, no big. Use no bias to avoid pow. But early steps m,g similar; okay. Use:
```
m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g; v=b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g; z -= lr*m/(sqrt(v)+eps)
```
Good.

Need clip finite:
```
step = m/(np.sqrt(v)+eps)
step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
max_step = np.max(np.abs(step))
if max_step > 10: step *= 10/max_step
z = z - lr*step
```
This stabilizes. Since step usually O(1), lr 0.05. If outlier, clip.

Softmax function:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z,float)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
       return np.zeros_like(z)+1/len(z)
    return e/s
```
z max subtract; if z all -inf? Not due clip.

Let's outline full code now mentally:

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _sp_next_fast_len
    from scipy.fft import rfft as _sp_rfft
    from scipy.fft import ifft as _sp_ifft
except Exception:
    def _sp_next_fast_len(n):
       m=1
       n=int(max(1,n))
       while m<n: m<<=1
       return m
    _sp_rfft = np.fft.rfft
    _sp_ifft = np.fft.ifft

def next_len(n): return _sp_next_fast_len(int(n))
_RFFT = _sp_rfft; _IFFT = _sp_ifft

def normalize_nonnegative(x): ...
def score_fft(a): ...
def make_generalized_p(n,p): ...
def make_modulated(base,freq,amp,phase): ...
def make_ramp(base,beta): ...
def make_noisy(base,rng,sigma): ...
def resample_sequence(a,n_new): ...
def build_candidate_pool(n,extra_arrays,seed): ...
def score_item_key(item): return item[0]
def score_candidates(pool): ...
def select_diverse(scored,k): ...

OPT_STAGES = (('above',1,0.15), ...)

def make_weights_from_b(b, mode, q, n): ...
def gradient_weights(A, w, n, L, phase): ...
def softmax_z(z): ...
def adam_update(z,g,m,v,t,lr,b1,b2,eps): ...

def optimize_sequence(a_init, budget_s, rng=None, stop_score=0.0):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a_init)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a,np.inf
    # compute initial score with score_fft
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_fft(a)
    init_score=best_score
    print(...)
    if budget_s <= 1.0 or not np.isfinite(best_score): return best_a,best_score
    L=next_len(3*n-2)
    P_len=2*n-1
    freq=np.arange(L//2+1)
    phase=np.exp(-2j*np.pi*freq*(P_len-1)/float(L))
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-35,50)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    total_frac=sum(f for _,_,f in OPT_STAGES)
    for mode,q,frac in OPT_STAGES:
        stage_budget = max(0.5, budget_s*frac/total_frac)
        stage_end = time.monotonic() + stage_budget
        # reset from best
        a_best=best_a
        z=np.log(np.maximum(a_best,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-35,50)
        m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
        lr = 0.03 if q < 16 else 0.015
        last_imp=0; iter=0; stag_limit=5000
        while time.monotonic() < stage_end:
            iter +=1
            a=softmax_z(z)
            A=_RFFT(a,L)
            b=_IFFT(A*A).real[:P_len]
            b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
            sum_a=float(a.sum())
            max_b=float(b.max())
            score = (2.0*n*max_b)/(sum_a*sum_a) if sum_a>0 else np.inf
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); last_imp=iter
            if stop_score > 0 and best_score < stop_score:
                break
            w=make_weights_from_b(b, mode, q, n)
            g=gradient_weights(A,w,n,L,phase)
            gy=a*(g - float(np.dot(a,g)))
            gy=np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)
            z,m,v,t = adam_update(z,gy,m,v,1? Need t variable. We initialized t=0. Define t=0.
            # clip step? adam_update includes
            z=np.clip(z, -35, 50); z-=z.mean()
            if iter - last_imp > 2000 and iter % 1000 == 0:
                z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n)
                z=np.clip(z,-35,50); z-=z.mean()
            if iter - last_imp > stag_limit:
                break
        # stage end
    return best_a, best_score
```
Need t variable. In adam_update, if no bias, t not needed; define `adam_update(z,g,m,v,lr,...)` no t. Simpler:
```
def adam_update(z,g,m,v,lr,b1,b2,eps):
   m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
   v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
   step = m / (np.sqrt(v)+eps)
   step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
   ms = np.max(np.abs(step))
   if ms > 10.0: step *= 10.0/ms
   z = z - lr*step
   return z,m,v
```
Use eps=1e-12.

But if g is zero, m decays, step small. Good.

`make_weights_from_b`: Need if mode='above'. Use n for c. For power q maybe 256; `np.power(r,q)` with q int. If q==1. Good.

`gradient_weights`: 
```
def gradient_weights(A,w,n,L,phase):
    P = n + (2*n-1) -1 # 3n-2
    W = _RFFT(w, L)
    Wr = W * phase
    c = _IFFT(A * Wr).real[:3*n-2]
    sl = c[n-1:2*n-1]
    g = 2.0 * sl[::-1]
    # clean
    g = np.where(g < 0.0, 0.0, g)
    # if any huge due issue, clip to finite
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g
```

def run_n_for_size(n, topk, slice_s, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, stop_score=1.48):
    extras=[]
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size>0:
       if best_a.size == n:
          extras.append(best_a.copy())
       else:
          extras.append(resample_sequence(best_a, n))
    pool=build_candidate_pool(n, extras, seed=int(n)+7)
    scored=score_candidates(pool)
    scored.sort(key=score_item_key)
    top=select_diverse(scored, max(1,topk))
    for init,s0 in top:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60: break
       alloc = min(slice_s, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30)
       if alloc < 30: break
       a_opt,s_opt=optimize_sequence(init, alloc, rng=rng, stop_score=stop_score)
       if finite and s_opt < best_score:
          best_score=s_opt; best_a=a_opt; print
    return best_a,best_score
```

"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _next_fast_len
    from scipy.fft import rfft as _rfft
    from scipy.fft import ifft as _ifft
except Exception:
    def _next_fast_len(n):
        n = int(max(1,n))
        p=1
        while p<n: p <<=1
        return p
    _rfft = np.fft.rfft
    _ifft = np.fft.ifft
...

`score_fft`:
```
def score_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-300: return inf
    L=_next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    A=_rfft(a,L)
    b=_ifft(A*A).real[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    mb=float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*mb/(s*s))
```
If a has negative? normalize first.

`normalize_nonnegative`: If x length 0, return empty? But propose expects nonempty. Use:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(x):
    a=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim !=1:
       a=a.ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if a.size==0: return np.zeros(1)
    if s <=1e-300:
       a=np.ones(a.size, dtype=float)
       s=float(a.size)
    return a/s
```
If input scalar? a.size 1, fine.

`make_generalized_p`: if n<1, return ones. For p maybe 0.0? recurrence with p=0 gives zero after first, normalize -> [1,0,...], bad. Avoid. Use max(p,1e-6).

`make_modulated`: Ensure base positive? Use normalize after. If base sum zero, uniform.
```
def make_modulated(base,freq,amp,phase):
 a=np.asarray(base,float).copy(); n=a.size
 if n==0: return a
 t=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/float(n)
 w=1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*t + phase)
 w=np.maximum(w,1e-6)
 return normalize_nonnegative(a*w)
```

`make_ramp`:
```
t=2*np.arange(n)/n -1
w=np.exp(beta*t)
return normalize_nonnegative(base*w)
```

`make_noisy_log(base,rng,sigma)`:
```
a=np.asarray(base,float)
if a.size==0: return a
noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=a.size))
return normalize_nonnegative(a*noise)
```

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n_new):
 a=np.asarray(a,float)
 n=a.size
 if n==0: return normalize_nonnegative(np.ones(n_new))
 if n==n_new: return normalize_nonnegative(a)
 loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
 if n>1:
   tx=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   ty=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
   val=np.interp(ty,tx,loga)
 else:
   val=np.full(n_new,loga[0])
 val -= float(np.max(val))
 out=np.exp(np.clip(val,-70,0))
 return normalize_nonnegative(out)
```

`build_candidate_pool`: Need avoid too many if n small? For n=2048, p list 15, mod 24, ramp 5, random 3 => ~50. OK.
```
def build_candidate_pool(n, extra_arrays, seed):
    pool=[]
    if n<1: return pool
    p_values = [0.50,0.52,0.54,0.56,0.58,0.60,0.63,0.66,0.70,0.75,0.80,0.90,1.00,1.20,1.50]
    if n <= 2048: p_values = p_values + [0.45,1.8]
    for p in p_values: pool.append((make_generalized_p(n,p), 'p:%.3f' % p))
    base_ps = [0.5,0.6,0.7]
    for pbase in base_ps:
       base=make_generalized_p(n,pbase)
       for freq in [1.0,2.0,4.0,7.0]:
          for amp in [0.3,0.6]:
             pool.append((make_modulated(base, freq, amp, 0.0),
                         'mod:%.3f:f%.1f:a%.1f' % (pbase, freq, amp)))
    base=make_generalized_p(n,0.5)
    for beta in [-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]: pool.append((make_ramp(base,beta),'ramp:%.1f'%beta))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for sigma in [0.2,0.5]: pool.append((make_noisy_log(base,rng,sigma),'rand:%.1f'%sigma))
    if extra_arrays:
       idx=0
       for ea in extra_arrays:
          if ea is None: continue
          arr=np.asarray(ea,float)
          if arr.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
             pool.append((normalize_nonnegative(arr), 'extra:%d' % idx))
             idx+=1
    return pool
```
Tag format with colon multiple; family = tag.split(':')[0] works: 'mod', 'p', etc. For `mod:0.5:f1.0:a0.3`, split first 'mod'. Good.

`score_candidates`:
```
def score_candidates(pool):
    scored=[]
    for a,tag in pool:
       a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
       s=score_fft(a)
       if np.isfinite(s):
          scored.append((float(s), a, tag))
    scored.sort(key=score_item_key)
    return scored
```

`select_diverse`:
```
def select_diverse(scored,k):
    if k<=0 or not scored: return []
    sel=[]; used=set()
    for s,a,tag in scored:
        fam=tag.split(':')[0]
        if fam in used: continue
        sel.append((a,float(s)))
        used.add(fam)
        if len(sel)>=k: return sel
    # fill
    for s,a,tag in scored:
        if len(sel)>=k: break
        # avoid exact duplicate? no
        sel.append((a,float(s)))
    return sel
```
If top k all same family, first pass adds one, second pass adds more. Good.

`make_weights_from_b`:
```
def make_weights_from_b(b, mode, q, n):
    b=np.asarray(b,float)
    if b.size==0: return b.copy()
    if mode == 'above':
       c=1.0/float(2*n-1)
       x=b-c
       x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
       if float(x.sum()) <= 1e-300:
          x=b*b
    else:
       x=b
    xmax=float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(xmax) or xmax <= 0.0:
       w=np.full(b.size,1.0/float(b.size))
       return w
    r=x/xmax
    if q <= 1:
       w=r
    else:
       with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
          w=np.power(r, int(q))
       w=np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       w=np.zeros(b.size)
       idx=int(np.argmax(x))
       w[idx]=1.0
       return w
    return w/s
```
If mode power and b has zeros, r zeros. Good.

`gradient_weights` as above. Need use `int(3*n-2)` slice stop. Ensure c length enough: `c = _ifft(A * Wr).real`; length L >=3n-2. `c_valid = c[:3*n-2]`.

`softmax_z`:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size==0: return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       return np.full(z.size,1.0/float(z.size))
    return e/s
```

`adam_update`:
```
def adam_update(z,g,m,v,lr,b1,b2,eps):
    m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*g
    v = b2*v + (1.0-b2)*(g*g)
    step = m / (np.sqrt(v) + eps)
    step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
    ms=float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
    if ms > 10.0:
       step = step * (10.0/ms)
    z = z - lr*step
    return z,m,v
```
If step all zero, ms=0. OK.

def run_n_for_size(n, topk, slice_s, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, stop_score=1.48):
    if n <=0: return best_a,best_score
    extras=[]
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a)>0:
       if len(best_a)==n:
          extras.append(best_a.copy())
       else:
          extras.append(resample_sequence(best_a,int(n)))
    pool=build_candidate_pool(n, extras, seed=int(n)*1000+17)
    scored=score_candidates(pool)
    if not scored: return best_a,best_score
    top=select_diverse(scored, int(max(1,topk)))
    print('n=%d top initial score=%.6f' % (n, top[0][1] if top else best_score))
    for init,s0 in top:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 40: break
       alloc=min(float(slice_s), rem-20)
       if alloc<30: break
       a_opt,s_opt=optimize_sequence(init, alloc, rng=rng, stop_score=stop_score)
       if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
          best_score=float(s_opt); best_a=a_opt
          print('updated best n=%d score=%.6f' % (n,best_score))
    return best_a,best_score
```

```python
"""
Optimizes nonnegative step-function heights to minimize
    2*n*max_conv / sum^2
by:
1) scanning one-sided generalized-polynomial and modulated families,
2) resampling promising shapes across lengths,
3) running log-domain Adam on a sequence of increasingly peak-oriented
   surrogate objectives computed with FFT-based convolution gradients.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _next_fast_len
    from scipy.fft import rfft as _rfft
    from scipy.fft import ifft as _ifft
except Exception:
    def _next_fast_len(n):
        n = int(max(1, n))
        p = 1
        while p < n:
            p <<= 1
        return p
    _rfft = np.fft.rfft
    _ifft = np.fft.ifft


def normalize_nonnegative(x):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    L = int(_next_fast_len(2 * n - 1))
    A = _rfft(a, L)
    b = _ifft(A * A).real[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    mb = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))


def make_generalized_p(n, p):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = float(max(1e-6, p))
    a = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (float(i - 1) + p) / float(i)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_modulated(base, freq, amp, phase):
    a = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    w = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * t + float(phase))
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a * w)


def make_ramp(base, beta):
    a = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    t = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n)) - 1.0
    w = np.exp(float(beta) * t)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a * w)


def make_noisy_log(base, rng, sigma):
    a = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=n))
    return normalize_nonnegative(a * noise)


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    n_new = int(max(1, n_new))
    if n == 0:
        return normalize_nonnegative(np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64))
    if n == n_new:
        return normalize_nonnegative(a)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    if n > 1:
        tx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        ty = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
        val = np.interp(ty, tx, loga)
    else:
        val = np.full(n_new, float(loga[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    val -= float(np.max(val))
    out = np.exp(np.clip(val, -70.0, 0.0))
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def build_candidate_pool(n, extra_arrays, seed):
    pool = []
    n = int(max(1, n))

    p_values = [
        0.50, 0.52, 0.54, 0.56, 0.58, 0.60, 0.63, 0.66,
        0.70, 0.75, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00, 1.20, 1.50
    ]
    if n <= 2048:
        p_values += [0.45, 1.80]

    for p in p_values:
        pool.append((make_generalized_p(n, p), "p:%.3f" % p))

    for pbase in (0.5, 0.6, 0.7):
        base = make_generalized_p(n, pbase)
        for freq in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 7.0):
            for amp in (0.3, 0.6):
                pool.append(
                    (
                        make_modulated(base, freq, amp, 0.0),
                        "mod:%.3f:f%.1f:a%.1f" % (pbase, freq, amp)
                    )
                )

    base = make_generalized_p(n, 0.5)
    for beta in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        pool.append((make_ramp(base, beta), "ramp:%.1f" % beta))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    for sigma in (0.2, 0.5):
        pool.append((make_noisy_log(base, rng, sigma), "rand:%.1f" % sigma))

    if extra_arrays:
        idx = 0
        for ea in extra_arrays:
            if ea is None:
                continue
            arr = np.asarray(ea, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                pool.append((normalize_nonnegative(arr), "extra:%d" % idx))
                idx += 1

    return pool


def score_item_key(item):
    return item[0]


def score_candidates(pool):
    scored = []
    for a, tag in pool:
        a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
        s = score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((float(s), a, tag))
    scored.sort(key=score_item_key)
    return scored


def select_diverse(scored, k):
    k = int(max(0, k))
    if k == 0 or not scored:
        return []
    selected = []
    used = set()
    for s, a, tag in scored:
        fam = tag.split(":")[0]
        if fam in used:
            continue
        selected.append((a, float(s)))
        used.add(fam)
        if len(selected) >= k:
            return selected
    for s, a, tag in scored:
        if len(selected) >= k:
            break
        selected.append((a, float(s)))
    return selected


OPT_STAGES = (
    ("above", 2, 0.30),
    ("power", 8, 0.25),
    ("power", 32, 0.20),
    ("power", 128, 0.15),
    ("softmax", 0, 0.10),
)


def make_weights_from_b(b, mode, q, n):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return b.copy()

    if mode == "softmax":
        bmax = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
            return np.full(b.size, 1.0 / float(b.size), dtype=np.float64)
        alpha = min(1.0e6, max(100.0, 30.0 / max(bmax, 1e-12)))
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            w = np.exp(alpha * (b - bmax))
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
            return w
        return w / s

    if mode == "above":
        c = 1.0 / float(2 * int(n) - 1)
        x = b - c
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        if float(x.sum()) <= 1e-300:
            x = b * b
    else:
        x = b

    xmax = float(x.max())
    if not np.isfinite(xmax) or xmax <= 0.0:
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / float(x.size), dtype=np.float64)

    r = x / xmax
    if q <= 1:
        w = r
    else:
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            w = np.power(r, int(q))
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(x))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def gradient_weights(A, w, n, L, phase):
    W = _rfft(w, L)
    Wr = W * phase
    c = _ifft(A * Wr).real[:3 * int(n) - 2]
    sl = c[int(n) - 1:2 * int(n) - 1]
    g = 2.0 * sl[::-1]
    g = np.where(g < 0.0, 0.0, g)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def adam_update(z, g, m, v, lr, b1, b2, eps):
    m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
    v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
    step = m / (np.sqrt(v) + eps)
    step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
    ms = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
    if ms > 10.0:
        step = step * (10.0 / ms)
    z = z - float(lr) * step
    return z, m, v


def optimize_sequence(a_init, budget_s, rng=None, stop_score=0.0):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a_init)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or float(budget_s) < 1.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)

    L = int(_next_fast_len(3 * n - 2))
    P_len = 2 * n - 1
    freq = np.arange(L // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * freq * float(P_len - 1) / float(L))

    print("    optimize n=%d init_score=%.6f budget=%.1f" % (n, best_score, float(budget_s)))

    total_frac = 0.0
    for _, _, f in OPT_STAGES:
        total_frac += float(f)
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        total_frac = 1.0

    stopped = False
    for mode, q, frac in OPT_STAGES:
        stage_budget = max(0.5, float(budget_s) * float(frac) / total_frac)
        stage_end = time.monotonic() + stage_budget

        z = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -35.0, 50.0)

        m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        if q <= 4:
            lr = 0.04
        elif q <= 32:
            lr = 0.03
        else:
            lr = 0.02

        it = 0
        last_improve = 0
        bad_count = 0

        while time.monotonic() < stage_end:
            it += 1

            a = softmax_z(z)
            A = _rfft(a, L)
            b = _ifft(A * A).real[:P_len]
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

            sa = float(a.sum())
            mb = float(b.max())
            if sa <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(mb):
                continue
            score = float(2.0 * n * mb / (sa * sa))

            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_improve = it
                bad_count = 0
            else:
                if np.isfinite(score) and score > best_score + 1e-7 and score > 1.01 * best_score:
                    bad_count += 1
                else:
                    bad_count = 0

            if stopped:
                break
            if stop_score > 0.0 and best_score < stop_score:
                stopped = True
                break

            if bad_count > 2500:
                z = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
                z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                z = np.clip(z, -35.0, 50.0)
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                bad_count = 0
                it = 0
                last_improve = 0

            w = make_weights_from_b(b, mode, q, n)
            g = gradient_weights(A, w, n, L, phase)
            gy = a * (g - float(np.dot(a, g)))
            gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)

            z, m, v = adam_update(z, gy, m, v, lr, 0.90, 0.999, 1e-12)
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
            z = np.clip(z, -35.0, 50.0)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))

            if (it - last_improve) > 2000 and (it % 1000) == 0:
                z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=n)
                z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
                z = np.clip(z, -35.0, 50.0)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))

            if (it - last_improve) > 6000:
                break

        print("      stage %s q=%d best=%.6f" % (mode, q, best_score))
        if stopped:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def run_n_for_size(n, topk, slice_s, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, stop_score=1.48):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    extras = []
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) > 0:
        if len(best_a) == n:
            extras.append(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
        else:
            extras.append(resample_sequence(best_a, n))

    pool = build_candidate_pool(n, extras, seed=int(n) * 1000 + 17)
    scored = score_candidates(pool)
    if not scored:
        return best_a, best_score

    top = select_diverse(scored, int(max(1, topk)))
    if top:
        print("  n=%d best_initial_score=%.6f" % (n, top[0][1]))

    for init, s0 in top:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 55.0:
            break
        alloc = min(float(slice_s), rem - 25.0)
        if alloc < 30.0:
            break

        a_opt, s_opt = optimize_sequence(init, alloc, rng=rng, stop_score=stop_score)
        if np.isfinite(s_opt) and float(s_opt) < best_score:
            best_score = float(s_opt)
            best_a = a_opt
            print("  updated best n=%d score=%.6f" % (n, best_score))

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Robust analytic fallback: truncated coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), ~1.57.
    best_a = make_generalized_p(4806, 0.5)
    best_score = score_fft(best_a)
    print("fallback score=%.6f" % best_score)

    # Optionally use the provided prior construction if it is already better.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim == 1 and 100 < parent.size < 20000:
            p = normalize_nonnegative(parent)
            ps = score_fft(p)
            if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_score:
                best_a = p
                best_score = float(ps)
                print("parent score=%.6f accepted" % best_score)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Coarse to fine search.  The smaller length helps transfer shape information
    # to larger lengths via log-interpolation.
    best_a, best_score = run_n_for_size(2048, 1, 80.0, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, 1.48)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        best_a, best_score = run_n_for_size(4806, 2, 380.0, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, 1.48)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        best_a, best_score = run_n_for_size(8192, 2, 350.0, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, 1.48)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 160.0:
        best_a, best_score = run_n_for_size(4806, 1, 130.0, best_a, best_score, rng, deadline, 1.48)

    best_a = normalize_nonnegative(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score > 2.0:
        best_a = make_generalized_p(4806, 0.5)
        best_score = score_fft(best_a)

    # Optional low-cost sanity print. Do not depend on this for correctness.
    try:
        if best_a.size <= 3000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
            final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
            ev = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
            if np.isfinite(ev):
                print("final internal evaluate_sequence=%.6f" % float(ev))
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
